do local function __modImpl()local aa=a.a()local ab=a.b()local ac=a.f()local ad=a.A()local ae=a.v()local af=a.u()local ah=a.H()
-- Twilight Reworked UI: target list (settings in its corner), button panel, steal bar
local S=ac.Theme local N=ac.New local U=UDim2.new local O=UDim2.fromOffset local F=UDim2.fromScale local V2=Vector2.new local fmt=string.format
local UIS=aa.Services.UserInputService local RS=aa.Services.RunService local LP=aa.LocalPlayer
local SB=ac.Fonts.SemiBold local GREEN=Color3.fromRGB(110,230,140) local WHITE=Color3.new(1,1,1)
local CENTER=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center local RIGHT=Enum.TextXAlignment.Right local CUT=Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
local M1,TOUCH,MOVE=Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1,Enum.UserInputType.Touch,Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
local function H(c)return ac.Hex(c)end
local function Holder(name,w)local f=N('Frame',{Name=name,Size=O(w,0),AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,BackgroundColor3=S.Window},ac.Overlay)
ac.Corner(f,8)ac.Stroke(f)ac.Shadow(f,10)table.insert(ac.Frames,f)f.Visible=ac.Shown return f end
local function Btn(p,props,r)local b=N('TextButton',props,p)ac.Corner(b,r or 6)return b end
local function Down(i)return i.UserInputType==M1 or i.UserInputType==TOUCH end

ScrollBarImageColor3=S.Accent,CanvasSize=O(0,0),AutomaticCanvasSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y,ScrollingDirection=Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y},body)
ac.List(s,4)ac.Padding(s,2,6,4,2)return s end

ac.Place('Twilight Targets',P,top,U(1,-266,0,60))

local ord,cnt={},0
local function Ord(e)local o=ord[e.Key]if not o then cnt+=1 o=cnt ord[e.Key]=o end return o end
local function Before(x,y)if af.Before(x,y)then return true end if af.Before(y,x)then return false end return Ord(x)<Ord(y)end
local rows={}local Kick
local function Row(i)local r={}local b=Btn(list,{Size=U(1,0,0,40),BackgroundColor3=S.Bar,LayoutOrder=i})r.B=b
r.St=N('UIStroke',{Color=S.Accent,Thickness=1.5,Enabled=false,ApplyStrokeMode=Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border},b)
r.A=N('TextLabel',{RichText=true,Position=O(10,4),Size=U(1,-16,0,18),FontFace=SB,TextSize=13,TextTruncate=CUT},b)
r.C=N('TextLabel',{RichText=true,Position=O(10,21),Size=U(1,-16,0,15),TextSize=11,TextColor3=S.Sub,TextTruncate=CUT},b)
b.Activated:Connect(function()if r.E then ad.Pick(r.E)Kick()end end)return r end
local function Fill()local items=ad.Ranked(true)table.sort(items,Before)
sub.Text=fmt('Steal Target \u{00B7} %d',#items)empty.Visible=#items==0
for i,e in items do local r=rows[i]if not r then r=Row(i)rows[i]=r end r.E=e r.B.Visible=true
local sel,stolen=ad.Selected(e),ad.Stolen(e)
r.B.BackgroundColor3=if sel then S.Bar:Lerp(S.Accent,0.3)else S.Bar r.St.Enabled=sel
r.A.Text=fmt('<font color="%s">#%d</font>  <font color="%s">%s</font>  %s',H(S.Accent),i,H(if stolen then S.Sub else GREEN),ah.Rate(e.Income or 0),e.Index)
local bits={'Slot'..tostring(e.Slot or'?')}local m=e.Mutation
if m and m~=''and m~='Default'and m~='None'and m~='Normal'then bits[#bits+1]=fmt('<font color="%s">%s</font>',H(ah.Mutations[m]or S.Sub),m)end
if e.OneOfOne==true then bits[#bits+1]='<font color="#ffcc33">1 OF 1</font>'end
if stolen then bits[#bits+1]=fmt('<font color="%s">being stolen</font>',H(S.Bad))end
r.C.Text=table.concat(bits,' \u{00B7} ')end
for i=#items+1,#rows do rows[i].B.Visible=false rows[i].E=nil end end
local pend=false
Kick=function()if pend then return end pend=true task.delay(0.15,function()pend=false if P.Visible and list.Visible then pcall(Fill)end end)end
aa.Clean(ae.Added:Connect(Kick))aa.Clean(ae.Removed:Connect(Kick))aa.Clean(ae.Updated:Connect(Kick))aa.Clean(ad.Changed:Connect(Kick))

local function Status()
if ad.Active then local p=math.clamp((os.clock()-ad.Start)/math.max(ad.GrabMin or 1,0.05),0,1)return fmt('Stealing %s %d%%',ad.Name,math.floor(p*100)),S.Accent end
local ch=LP.Character local hum=ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass'Humanoid'local g=ad.GummyLeft()
if LP:GetAttribute'BlockTools'==true or g>0 then return(if g>0 then fmt('Gummy blocked %.1fs',g)else'Gummy blocked'),S.Warn end
if hum and ad.Ragdolled(hum)then local t=ad.RagdollLeft()return(if t>0 then fmt('Ragdolled %.1fs',t)else'Ragdolled'),S.Warn end
local at=ad.Attempt if at then return(if at.Confirmed then'Confirming delivery'else'Confirming grab'),S.Good end
if ad.Busy or(ad.Smart and ad.Smart.Player)then return ad.Status,S.Sub end
local st=ab.Modules.AutoSteal if not(st and st.Enabled)then return'Auto Steal is off',S.Faint end
return'Waiting for target',S.Sub end

local function Switch(f,get,set,x)local b=N('TextButton',{AnchorPoint=V2(1,0),Position=U(1,x or-8,0,5),Size=O(34,18),BackgroundColor3=S.Off},f)ac.Corner(b)

local function Num(v,st)st=st or 1 if st<1 then return fmt('%.'..math.max(0,math.ceil(-math.log10(st)-1e-9))..'f',v)end return tostring(math.floor(v+0.5))end
local drag
local function SetFromX(d,x)local o=d.o local w=math.max(d.bar.AbsoluteSize.X,1)local t=math.clamp((x-d.bar.AbsolutePosition.X)/w,0,1)local st=o.Step or 1
local v=math.clamp(math.floor((o.Min+(o.Max-o.Min)*t)/st+0.5)*st,o.Min,o.Max)if math.abs(v-o.Value)>1e-9 then o:SetValue(v)end end
aa.Clean(UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)if drag and(i.UserInputType==MOVE or i.UserInputType==TOUCH)then SetFromX(drag,i.Position.X)end end))
aa.Clean(UIS.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)if Down(i)then drag=nil end end))
local function Option(par,o)local f
if o.Kind=='Toggle'then f=Line(par)Name(f,o.Name)o.Render=Switch(f,function()return o.Enabled end,function()o:Toggle()end)
elseif o.Kind=='Slider'then f=Line(par,42)Name(f,o.Name,96)
local val=N('TextLabel',{AnchorPoint=V2(1,0),Position=U(1,-10,0,0),Size=O(86,28),TextSize=12,TextColor3=S.Sub,TextXAlignment=RIGHT},f)
local bar=N('TextButton',{Position=O(10,29),Size=U(1,-20,0,6),BackgroundColor3=S.Track},f)ac.Corner(bar)
local fill=N('Frame',{Size=F(0,1),BackgroundColor3=S.Accent},bar)ac.Corner(fill)
local d={o=o,bar=bar}bar.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)if Down(i)then drag=d SetFromX(d,i.Position.X)end end)
o.Render=function()fill.Size=F(math.clamp((o.Value-o.Min)/math.max(o.Max-o.Min,1e-9),0,1),1)val.Text=Num(o.Value,o.Step)..(o.Suffix or'')end o.Render()
elseif o.Kind=='Dropdown'then f=Line(par)Name(f,o.Name,112)local b=Pill(f)
b.Activated:Connect(function()local i=table.find(o.List,o.Value)or 0 o:SetValue(o.List[i%#o.List+1])end)
o.Render=function()b.Text=tostring(o.Value)end o.Render()
elseif o.Kind=='TextBox'then f=Line(par)Name(f,o.Name,132)
local t=N('TextBox',{AnchorPoint=V2(1,0),Position=U(1,-6,0,4),Size=O(122,20),BackgroundColor3=S.Field,TextSize=11,Text=tostring(o.Value or''),PlaceholderText=o.Placeholder or'',ClearTextOnFocus=false},f)ac.Corner(t,5)
t.FocusLost:Connect(function()o:SetValue(t.Text)end)o.Render=function()if not t:IsFocused()then t.Text=tostring(o.Value or'')end end
else return end
o.Root=f if o.Visible==false then f.Visible=false end end

local function BindLine(par,m)local f=Line(par)Name(f,'Keybind',112)local b=Pill(f)

local painters={}

else ps[#ps+1]=Switch(f,function()return m.Enabled end,function()m:Toggle()end,-30)end

ex.Activated:Connect(function()box.Visible=not box.Visible ex.Text=if box.Visible then'v'else'>'end)
painters[m]=function()nm.Text=label()for _,p in ps do p()end end end

for _,m in c.Modules do if m~=M.Teleport and m~=M.AutoSteal and m.Name~='Menu'then Feature(m,false)end end end end

local lastOn=0
local function Target()local e=(ad.Attempt and ad.Attempt.Entity)or ad.PickedEntity()or(ad.Auto and ae.Get(ad.Auto))if e and e.Index==ad.Name then return e end return nil end
aa.Clean(RS.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
